# Plain-language sweep — jargon still undefined in user-facing text

Generated 2026-10-21 by `node scripts/plain-language.mjs --sweep docs/PLAIN_LANGUAGE_SWEEP.md` (docs/UX_AUDIT_AND_PLAN.md §8.2, §8.3). This file is regenerated, not edited: reword the copy or define the term in the package, rerun the script, and the row disappears. Every hit is a string literal, template literal, JSX text, SVG text or Cedar reason a founder can read; identifiers, enum keys, class lists, import paths, tests, stories and fixtures are not scanned.

Scope: `apps/web`, `packages/dugout`, `packages/widgets`, `packages/modules`, `packages/agents/src`, `apps/composer`, `packages/journey`, `packages/models`, `packages/db/src/seed`, `apps/gateway/src`, `packages/policy/src`, `packages/sandbox/src`, `packages/connectors/src`, `packages/agents/src/archetypes.ts`, `packages/agents/src/templates`, `packages/models/src/mock` (1025 files).

**3181 hit(s) in 13 package(s): 271 replace, 2909 define, 1 leak.** CI (`plain-language` job) reports these without failing until the package sweep lands; from then on it fails while the number is above zero. Gate 2 also wants no `plain-allow` on a replace-tier term: 0 silenced today.

## Tiers

- **replace** fails on sight: say the plain thing (Appendix E of the plan has the replacement for each word).
- **define** passes when the same file wraps the word in `Term` (its id as a string, or the word as the children), holds it in a `hint` / `definition` / `tooltip` / `help` / `caption` / `footer` / `plain` prop, a TermDefinition's `word` / `short` / `long`, an explainer's `purpose` / `watchFor` (in a file that names a `screenId`) or inside an `InfoTip` or `DefinitionCard`, writes a definition sentence (the word followed by a colon, "is", "are" or "means"), or mounts an `Explainer`. Guidance-off rendering changes nothing here: the definition exists in the file either way.
- **leak** is a developer note that shipped as copy ("Mock citation", "replace with", "See note.", "TODO"). Delete or rewrite it.

## How to read a hit

- `file:line:term:tier` is printed by the script; the tables above add the source line, and group define-tier hits by file and term because one definition clears them all.
- Internal identifiers (`acquisition_cost`, `--kit-primary`, `routes.pipeline`, `model: "..."`) are never hits; only the copy beside them is.
- A sentence that must name a word on purpose gets `plain-allow` in a comment on the same or the previous line. Never on a replace-tier word (Gate 2), and never to hide copy.
- Allowed false friends: "business model" and "revenue model", "Live sessions" and a sign-in session, a bank branch, an insurance or privacy policy, a web form, "lead" as a verb, a person's health, "fit" as a verb, "tested" and "proven" as adjectives, customer ratings.
- Agent templates, archetype task titles and option labels, and the mock completions count: what an agent is told to say, it says, and the mock is what the founder reads on the golden path.
- Developer documents (everything under `docs/`, `.claude/` and `.github/`, and README / CLAUDE / AGENTS files) are not product copy and are not scanned; other Markdown under a root is read as prose.
- The hit counts move as copy changes: the next run after a package's sweep should show that package's row gone, and Gate 2 wants the UI roots under 300.
